# `openssl cmp` (apps/cmp.c, with apps/lib/cmp_mock_srv.c): run with upstream's tool on the
# reference, then ours on rustyssl and on the reference (cargo xtask test-apps); output and
# exit codes must match. The mock server answers in-process (-use_mock_srv) or over HTTP on
# loopback (-port, the `&` lines). Messages carry random nonces and transaction IDs, so no
# full session runs at -verbosity 7 or more, and saved messages are only read back.
# The credentials are upstream's (test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/);
# data/cmp/cmp.cnf holds the configuration-file cases (and paths that need it: -ref_cert).
#
# Usage errors.
cmp 
cmp -help
cmp days 1
cmp -dayss
cmp -days a/
cmp -days 3.14
cmp -days 314_+
cmp -days 2147483648
cmp -verbosity 9 -cmd ir
cmp -cmd ir -verbosity 9
cmp -cmd ir -verbosity -1
cmp -keep_alive 3
cmp -popo 3
cmp -popo -2
cmp -revreason 7
cmp -revreason 11
cmp -revreason -2
cmp -cmd ir extra
cmp -server=
cmp -cmd ir
cmp -cmd ir -proxy 127.0.0.1:1 -no_proxy localhost -reqout_only {dir}/cmp.ro.der -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem
# What excludes what.
cmp -server 127.0.0.1:1 -use_mock_srv -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem
cmp -port 18840 -server 127.0.0.1:1
cmp -port 18840 -use_mock_srv
cmp -port 18840 -reqin x.der
cmp -port 18840 -reqout x.der
cmp -port 18840 -rspin x.der
cmp -port 18840 -rspout x.der
cmp -port 18840 -tls_used
cmp -tls_used -use_mock_srv -use_mock_srv -srv_ref mock -srv_secret pass:test -srv_cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.crt -srv_key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.key -srv_trusted {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_root.crt -no_check_time -rsp_cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_only.crt -rsp_key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -rsp_crl {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newcrl.pem -rsp_capubs {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/trusted.crt -rsp_extracerts {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_issuing.crt -rsp_newwithnew {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newWithNew.pem -rsp_newwithold {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newWithOld.pem -rsp_oldwithnew {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/oldWithNew.pem -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem
cmp -tls_trusted x.pem -use_mock_srv -use_mock_srv -srv_ref mock -srv_secret pass:test -srv_cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.crt -srv_key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.key -srv_trusted {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_root.crt -no_check_time -rsp_cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_only.crt -rsp_key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -rsp_crl {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newcrl.pem -rsp_capubs {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/trusted.crt -rsp_extracerts {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_issuing.crt -rsp_newwithnew {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newWithNew.pem -rsp_newwithold {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newWithOld.pem -rsp_oldwithnew {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/oldWithNew.pem -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem
cmp -tls_host localhost -tls_used -use_mock_srv -srv_ref mock -srv_secret pass:test -srv_cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.crt -srv_key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.key -srv_trusted {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_root.crt -no_check_time -rsp_cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_only.crt -rsp_key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -rsp_crl {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newcrl.pem -rsp_capubs {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/trusted.crt -rsp_extracerts {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_issuing.crt -rsp_newwithnew {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newWithNew.pem -rsp_newwithold {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newWithOld.pem -rsp_oldwithnew {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/oldWithNew.pem -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem
cmp -reqout_only {dir}/cmp.ro.der -port 18840
cmp -reqout_only {dir}/cmp.ro.der -reqout {dir}/x.der -rspin {dir}/y.der -rspout {dir}/z.der -use_mock_srv -srv_ref mock -srv_secret pass:test -srv_cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.crt -srv_key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.key -srv_trusted {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_root.crt -no_check_time -rsp_cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_only.crt -rsp_key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -rsp_crl {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newcrl.pem -rsp_capubs {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/trusted.crt -rsp_extracerts {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_issuing.crt -rsp_newwithnew {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newWithNew.pem -rsp_newwithold {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/newWithOld.pem -rsp_oldwithnew {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/oldWithNew.pem -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem

cmp -server 127.0.0.1:18845/pkix/ -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem -keep_alive 0 -verbosity 4
# TLS to a server (s_server with upstream's mock TLS credentials). No TLS front for the mock
# server exists, so a handshake that succeeds meets s_server sending a fixed HTTP response that
# isn't CMP; when it arrives relative to cmp's request varies (a content-type error, or a broken
# pipe or reset while writing), so those two lines (the second authenticates the client: its
# chain is built from -tls_extra and -tls_trusted, which has the client's root too) compare only
# exit codes. The server's verification failures (host, trust) are compared in full.
~s_server -accept 127.0.0.1:0 -naccept 1 -cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.crt -key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.key -ign_eof < {tests}/data/cmp/text-response.txt || cmp -server https://127.0.0.1:{port}/pkix/ -tls_used -tls_trusted {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/trusted.crt -tls_host server.example -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem
s_server -accept 127.0.0.1:0 -naccept 1 -cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.crt -key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.key || cmp -server https://127.0.0.1:{port}/pkix/ -tls_used -tls_trusted {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/trusted.crt -tls_host wrong.example -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem
s_server -accept 127.0.0.1:0 -naccept 1 -cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.crt -key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.key || cmp -server 127.0.0.1:{port} -tls_used -tls_trusted {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_root.crt -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem
~s_server -accept 127.0.0.1:0 -naccept 1 -cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.crt -key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/server.key -Verify 1 -CAfile {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_root.crt -ign_eof < {tests}/data/cmp/text-response.txt || cmp -server 127.0.0.1:{port} -path pkix/ -tls_used -tls_cert {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer.crt -tls_key {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer.key -tls_extra {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_issuing.crt -tls_trusted {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/trusted.crt,{src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/signer_root.crt -tls_host server.example -ref mock -secret pass:test -cmd ir -subject /CN=test -newkey {src}/test/recipes/80-test_cmp_http_data/Mock/new.key -certout {dir}/cmp.cert.pem
